PREFACE 


This brochure contains an index to the Gazette of India for 
the month of July , 1956 . Notifications relating to Acts , Ordiy 
Dances and Regulations (Part II, Section 1); Bills and Reports of 
Select Committees on Bills ( Part II, Section 2 ); and Statutor 
Rules and Orders ( Part II , Sections 3 and 4) have been indexed 
exhaustively . As regards other Notifications (Part I, Sections i 
and 3, Part III, Section I) only items of importance from 
Members view point have been indexed . References to the 
Parts and Sections of Gazette along with the page numbers have 
been given in the index for facility of reference. 


NEW DELHI, 
The 18th December, 1956 . 


M . N . KAUL , 

Secretary
